scipy uses (x,y,z,w)\\\\n    return r.as_rotvec().astype(np.float32)\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef obs_to_batch(obs, task_text, tokenizer, device):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Convert LIBERO env observation to model input batch.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    # Images: (H, W, 3) uint8 -> (1, 3, H, W) float [0,1]\\\\n    img1 = obs[\\\\\\\"pixels\\\\\\\"][\\\\\\\"image\\\\\\\"]  # agentview\\\\n    img2 = obs[\\\\\\\"pixels\\\\\\\"][\\\\\\\"image2\\\\\\\"]  # wrist\\\\n    \\\\n    img1_t = torch.from_numpy(np.asarray(img1)).permute(2, 0, 1).float() / 255.0\\\\n    img2_t = torch.from_numpy(np.asarray(img2)).permute(2, 0, 1).float() / 255.0\\\\n    \\\\n    # State: eef_pos(3) + axis_angle(3) + gripper_qpos(2) = 8D\\\\n    eef_pos = obs[\\\\\\\"robot_state\\\\\\\"][\\\\\\\"eef\\\\\\\"][\\\\\\\"pos\\\\\\\"]  # (3,)\\\\n    eef_quat = obs[\\\\\\\"robot_state\\\\\\\"][\\\\\\\"eef\\\\\\\"][\\\\\\\"quat\\\\\\\"]  # (4,) xyzw\\\\n    gripper_qpos = obs[\\\\\\\"robot_state\\\\\\\"][\\\\\\\"gripper\\\\\\\"][\\\\\\\"qpos\\\\\\\"]  # (2,)\\\\n    \\\\n    axis_angle = quat_to_axis_angle(eef_quat)\\\\n    state = np.concatenate([eef_pos, axis_angle, gripper_qpos]).astype(np.float32)\\\\n    state_t = torch.from_numpy(state).unsqueeze(0)\\\\n    \\\\n    batch = {\\\\n        \\\\\\\"observation.images.image\\\\\\\": img1_t.unsqueeze(0),\\\\n        \\\\\\\"observation.images.image2\\\\\\\": img2_t.unsqueeze(0),\\\\n        \\\\\\\"observation.state\\\\\\\": state_t,\\\\n        \\\\\\\"task\\\\\\\": [task_text],\\\\n    }\\\\n    return preprocess_batch(batch, tokenizer, device=device)\\\\n\\\\n\\\\n@torch.no_grad()\\\\ndef predict_actions(policy, batch):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Predict action chunk from preprocessed batch.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    images, img_masks = policy.prepare_images(batch)\\\\n    state = policy.prepare_state(batch)\\\\n    lang_tokens = batch[\\\\\\\"observation.language.tokens\\\\\\\"]\\\\n    lang_masks = batch[\\\\\\\"observation.language.attention_mask\\\\\\\"]\\\\n    \\\\n    actions = policy.model.sample_actions(\\\\n        images, img_masks, lang_tokens, lang_masks, state\\\\n    )\\\\n    action_dim = policy.config.action_feature.shape[0]\\\\n    return actions[0, :, :action_dim]  # observation_width=256,\\n            observation_height=256,\\n            control_mode=\\\"relative\\\",\\n            init_states=True,\\n            episode_index=0,\\n            n_envs=1,\\n        )\\n        \\n        for ep in range(eps_per_task):\\n            obs, info = env.reset(seed=42 + ep)\\n            action_chunk = None\\n            action_idx = 0\\n            success = False\\n            \\n            for step in range(max_steps):\\n                if action_chunk is None or action_idx >= action_horizon:\\n                    batch = obs_to_batch(obs, task_text,       if terminated or truncated:\\n                    break\\n            \\n            task_successes.append(success)\\n            status = \\\"SUCCESS\\\" if success else \\\"FAIL\\\"\\n len(task_successes),\\n            \\\"success_rate\\\": sr,\\n        }\\n        total_success += sum(task_successes)\\n        total_eps += len(task_successes)\\n        print(f\\\"  Task {task_id}   \\n    output = {\\n        \\\"checkpoint\\\": str(ckpt_path),\\n        \\\"config\\\": config_path,\\n        \\\"total_success\\\": total_success,\\n        \\\"total_episodes\\\": total_eps,\\n        \\\"overall_success_rate\\\": overall_sr,\\n        \\\"max_steps\\\": max_steps,\\n        \\\"action_horizon\\\": action_horizon,\\n        \\\"per_task\\\": results_per_task,\\n    }\\n json.dump(output, f, indent=2)\\n    print(f\\\"\\\\nResults saved to {out_path}\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"\\\\n{'='*50}\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"OVERALL SR: {overall_sr:.2%} {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01QLmHFYwytNSoqQTzU32NPE","type":"tool_result","content":"{\"result\":\"{\\\"success\\\": true, \\\"data\\\": {\\\"stdout\\\": \\\"# LIBERO baseline: SmolVLA + LoRA fine-tuning, no IMM.\\\\n# Matches SmolVLA paper protocol for LIBERO benchmark.\\\\n#\\\\n# Official SmolVLA LIBERO results (paper): 87.3% avg\\\\n#   Spatial=90%, Object=96%, Goal=92%, Long=71%\\\\n#\\\\n# Training: lerobot-train uses policy.type=smolvla with train_expert_only=True\\\\n# We use LoRA fine-tuning from smolvla_base for IMM compatibility.\\\\n\\\\nmodel:\\\\n  name: smolvla\\\\n  pretrained: \\\\\\\"/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache/lerobot/smolvla_base\\\\\\\"\\\\n  lora:\\\\n    rank: 32\\\\n    alpha: 16\\\\n    dropout: 0.05\\\\n    target_modules_regex: \\\\\\\"(vlm_with_expert\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\.lm_expert\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\..*\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\.(q|v)_proj|(state_proj|action_in_proj|action_out_proj|action_time_mlp_in|action_time_mlp_out))\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\nimm:\\\\n  enabled: false\\\\n\\\\ndataset:\\\\n  repo_id: \\\\\\\"HuggingFaceVLA/libero\\\\\\\"\\\\n  features:\\\\n    state_dim: 8       # eef_pos(3) + axis_angle(3) + gripper_qpos(2)\\\\n    action_dim: 7      # 6D EEF delta + 1D gripper\\\\n    cameras:\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"observation.images.image\\\\\\\"        # agentview\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"observation.images.image2\\\\\\\"       # wrist (robot0_eye_in_hand)\\\\n\\\\ntraining:\\\\n  total_steps: 100000\\\\n  batch_size: 4\\\\n  eval_batch_size: 1\\\\n  num_workers: 4\\\\n  lr: 1.0e-4\\\\n  betas: [0.9, 0.95]\\\\n  weight_decay: 1.0e-10\\\\n  warmup_steps: 1000\\\\n  decay_lr: 2.5e-6\\\\n  grad_clip: 10.0\\\\n  eval_every: 5000\\\\n  save_every: 10000\\\\n  log_every: 50\\\\n  output_dir: \\\\\\\"checkpoints/libero_baseline\\\\\\\"\\\\n  seed: 42\\\\n\\\\nevaluation:\\\\n  # Offline eval metrics (perturbation robustness)\\\\n  perturbations: [0.0, 0.01, 0.02, 0.05]\\\\n  pruning_ratio: 0.5\\\\n  max_eval_batches: 50\\\\n\\\\n# LIBERO sim eval protocol (for eval_success_rate_libero.py)\\\\nlibero_eval:\\\\n  suites:\\\\n    - libero_spatial\\\\n    - libero_object\\\\n    - libero_goal\\\\n    - libero_10\\\\n  n_episodes_per_task: 10    # 10 tasks/suite × 10 eps = 100 eps/suite\\\\n  control_mode: \\\\\\\"relative\\\\\\\"\\\\n  cameras: \\\\\\\"agentview_image,robot0_eye_in_hand_image\\\\\\\"\\\\n  max_steps:\\\\n    libero_spatial: 280\\\\n    libero_object: 280\\\\n    libero_goal: 300\\\\n    libero_10: 520\\\\n\\\", \\\"stderr\\\": \\\"\\\", \\\"return_code\\\": 0}, \\\"error\\\": null, \\\"metadata\\\": {\\\"elapsed_seconds\\\": 0.37}}\"}"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"6fce3b75-6795-41b2-890d-533edd1dff4e","uuid":"a3df95d9-7fe4-4f12-a4f9-a6123ec858fa","timestamp":"2026-05-16T21:31:36.257Z","tool_use_result":{"content":"{\"result\":\"{\\\"success\\\": true, \\\"data\\\": {\\\"stdout\\\": \\\"# LIBERO baseline: SmolVLA + LoRA fine-tuning, no IMM.\\\\n# Matches SmolVLA paper protocol for LIBERO benchmark.\\\\n#\\\\n# Official SmolVLA LIBERO results (paper): 87.3% avg\\\\n#   Spatial=90%, Object=96%, Goal=92%, Long=71%\\\\n#\\\\n# Training: lerobot-train uses policy.type=smolvla with train_expert_only=True\\\\n# We use LoRA fine-tuning from smolvla_base for IMM compatibility.\\\\n\\\\nmodel:\\\\n  name: smolvla\\\\n  pretrained: \\\\\\\"/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache/lerobot/smolvla_base\\\\\\\"\\\\n  lora:\\\\n    rank: 32\\\\n    alpha: 16\\\\n    dropout: 0.05\\\\n    target_modules_regex: \\\\\\\"(vlm_with_expert\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\.lm_expert\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\..*\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\.(q|v)_proj|(state_proj|action_in_proj|action_out_proj|action_time_mlp_in|action_time_mlp_out))\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\nimm:\\\\n  enabled: false\\\\n\\\\ndataset:\\\\n  repo_id: \\\\\\\"HuggingFaceVLA/libero\\\\\\\"\\\\n  features:\\\\n    state_dim: 8       # eef_pos(3) + axis_angle(3) + gripper_qpos(2)\\\\n    action_dim: 7      # 6D EEF delta + 1D gripper\\\\n    cameras:\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"observation.images.image\\\\\\\"        # agentview\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"observation.images.image2\\\\\\\"       # wrist (robot0_eye_in_hand)\\\\n\\\\ntraining:\\\\n  total_steps: 100000\\\\n  batch_size: 4\\\\n  eval_batch_size: 1\\\\n  num_workers: 4\\\\n  lr: 1.0e-4\\\\n  betas: [0.9, 0.95]\\\\n  weight_decay: 1.0e-10\\\\n  warmup_steps: 1000\\\\n  decay_lr: 2.5e-6\\\\n  grad_clip: 10.0\\\\n  eval_every: 5000\\\\n  save_every: 10000\\\\n  log_every: 50\\\\n  output_dir: \\\\\\\"checkpoints/libero_baseline\\\\\\\"\\\\n  seed: 42\\\\n\\\\nevaluation:\\\\n  # Offline eval metrics (perturbation robustness)\\\\n  perturbations: [0.0, 0.01, 0.02, 0.05]\\\\n  pruning_ratio: 0.5\\\\n  max_eval_batches: 50\\\\n\\\\n# LIBERO sim eval protocol (for eval_success_rate_libero.py)\\\\nlibero_eval:\\\\n  suites:\\\\n    - libero_spatial\\\\n    - libero_object\\\\n    - libero_goal\\\\n    - libero_10\\\\n  n_episodes_per_task: 10    # 10 tasks/suite × 10 eps = 100 eps/suite\\\\n  control_mode: \\\\\\\"relative\\\\\\\"\\\\n  cameras: \\\\\\\"agentview_image,robot0_eye_in_hand_image\\\\\\\"\\\\n  max_steps:\\\\n    libero_spatial: 280\\\\n    libero_object: 280\\\\n    libero_goal: 300\\\\n    libero_10: 520\\\\n\\\", \\\"stderr\\\": \\\"\\\", \\\"return_code\\\": 0}, \\\"error\\\": null, \\\"metadata\\\": {\\\"elapsed_seconds\\\": 0.37}}\"}","structuredContent":{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"# LIBERO baseline: SmolVLA + LoRA fine-tuning, no IMM.\\n# Matches SmolVLA paper protocol for LIBERO benchmark.\\n#\\n# Official SmolVLA LIBERO results (paper): 87.3% avg\\n#   Spatial=90%, Object=96%, Goal=92%, Long=71%\\n#\\n# Training: lerobot-train uses policy.type=smolvla with train_expert_only=True\\n# We use LoRA fine-tuning from smolvla_base for IMM compatibility.\\n\\nmodel:\\n  name: smolvla\\n  pretrained: \\\"/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache/lerobot/smolvla_base\\\"\\n  lora:\\n    rank: 32\\n    alpha: 16\\n    dropout: 0.05\\n    target_modules_regex: \\\"(vlm_with_expert\\\\\\\\.lm_expert\\\\\\\\..*\\\\\\\\.(q|v)_proj|(state_proj|action_in_proj|action_out_proj|action_time_mlp_in|action_time_mlp_out))\\\"\\n\\nimm:\\n  enabled: false\\n\\ndataset:\\n  repo_id: \\\"HuggingFaceVLA/libero\\\"\\n  features:\\n    state_dim: 8       # eef_pos(3) + axis_angle(3) + gripper_qpos(2)\\n    action_dim: 7      # 6D EEF delta + 1D gripper\\n    cameras:\\n      - \\\"observation.images.image\\\"        # agentview\\n      - \\\"observation.images.image2\\\"       # wrist (robot0_eye_in_hand)\\n\\ntraining:\\n  total_steps: 100000\\n  batch_size: 4\\n  eval_batch_size: 1\\n  num_workers: 4\\n  lr: 1.0e-4\\n  betas: [0.9, 0.95]\\n  weight_decay: 1.0e-10\\n  warmup_steps: 1000\\n  decay_lr: 2.5e-6\\n  grad_clip: 10.0\\n  eval_every: 5000\\n  save_every: 10000\\n  log_every: 50\\n  output_dir: \\\"checkpoints/libero_baseline\\\"\\n  seed: 42\\n\\nevaluation:\\n  # Offline eval metrics (perturbation robustness)\\n  perturbations: [0.0, 0.01, 0.02, 0.05]\\n  pruning_ratio: 0.5\\n  max_eval_batches: 50\\n\\n# LIBERO sim eval protocol (for eval_success_rate_libero.py)\\nlibero_eval:\\n  suites:\\n    - libero_spatial\\n    - libero_object\\n    - libero_goal\\n    - libero_10\\n  n_episodes_per_task: 10    # 10 tasks/suite × 10 eps = 100 eps/suite\\n  control_mode: \\\"relative\\\"\\n  cameras: \\\"agentview_image,robot0_eye_in_hand_image\\\"\\n  max_steps:\\n    libero_spatial: 280\\n    libero_object: 280\\n    libero_goal: 300\\n    libero_10: 520\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.37}}"}}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_016sninbeKGrn7Fwmv4NG9za","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01U85YApgBHndHWAVv6ZZC2z","name":"mcp__agent-ml-research__ssh_execute","input":{"server":"cqa1-33015","command":"cat > /root/autodl-tmp/importance_margin_vla_compress/eval_libero_fullft_100k.py << 'PYEOF'\n\"\"\"Eval Full FT 100K checkpoint on libero_object, libero_spatial, libero_goal.\n10 episodes per task, 10 tasks per suite = 100 episodes per suite.\nRun on cuda:0.\n\"\"\"\nimport os\nimport sys\nimport json\nimport time\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nos.environ[\"CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES\"] = \"0\"\nos.environ[\"MUJOCO_GL\"] = \"egl\"\nos.environ[\"HF_HOME\"] = \"/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache\"\nos.environ[\"TRANSFORMERS_OFFLINE\"] = \"1\"\nos.environ[\"HF_HUB_OFFLINE\"] = \"1\"\n\nPROJECT_DIR = Path(\"/root/autodl-tmp/importance_margin_vla_compress\")\nsys.path.insert(0, str(PROJECT_DIR))\n\nimport numpy as np\nimport torch\nfrom scipy.spatial.transform import Rotation\n\nfrom test_mode_collapse import load_model, load_config\nfrom train import get_tokenizer_from_policy, preprocess_batch\n\n\ndef quat_to_axis_angle(quat):\n    r = Rotation.from_quat(quat)\n    return r.as_rotvec().astype(np.float32)\n\n\ndef obs_to_batch(obs, task_text, tokenizer, device):\n    img1 = obs[\"pixels\"][\"image\"]\n    img2 = obs[\"pixels\"][\"image2\"]\n    img1_t = torch.from_numpy(np.asarray(img1)).permute(2, 0, 1).float() / 255.0\n    img2_t = torch.from_numpy(np.asarray(img2)).permute(2, 0, 1).float() / 255.0\n\n    eef_pos = obs[\"robot_state\"][\"eef\"][\"pos\"]\n    eef_quat = obs[\"robot_state\"][\"eef\"][\"quat\"]\n    gripper_qpos = obs[\"robot_state\"][\"gripper\"][\"qpos\"]\n\n    axis_angle = quat_to_axis_angle(eef_quat)\n    state = np.concatenate([eef_pos, axis_angle, gripper_qpos]).astype(np.float32)\n    state_t = torch.from_numpy(state).unsqueeze(0)\n\n    batch = {\n        \"observation.images.image\": img1_t.unsqueeze(0),\n        \"observation.images.image2\": img2_t.unsqueeze(0),\n        \"observation.state\": state_t,\n        \"task\": [task_text],\n    }\n    return preprocess_batch(batch, tokenizer, device=device)\n\n\n@torch.no_grad()\ndef predict_actions(policy, batch):\n    images, img_masks = policy.prepare_images(batch)\n    state = policy.prepare_state(batch)\n    lang_tokens = batch[\"observation.language.tokens\"]\n    lang_masks = batch[\"observation.language.attention_mask\"]\n    actions = policy.model.sample_actions(\n        images, img_masks, lang_tokens, lang_masks, state\n    )\n    action_dim = policy.config.action_feature.shape[0]\n    return actions[0, :, :action_dim]\n\n\nSUITE_MAX_STEPS = {\n    \"libero_object\": 280,\n    \"libero_spatial\": 280,\n    \"libero_goal\": 300,\n}\nACTION_HORIZON = 50\nEPS_PER_TASK = 10\n\n\ndef eval_suite(suite_name, policy, tokenizer, device):\n    from libero.libero import benchmark\n    from lerobot.envs.libero import LiberoEnv, get_task_init_states\n\n    max_steps = SUITE_MAX_STEPS[suite_name]\n    suite = benchmark.get_benchmark_dict()[suite_name]()\n    n_tasks = len(suite.tasks)\n\n    results_per_task = {}\n    total_success = 0\n    total_eps = 0\n    t0 = time.time()\n\n    for task_id in range(n_tasks):\n        task_text = suite.tasks[task_id].language + \"\\n\"\n        task_successes = []\n\n        env = LiberoEnv(\n            task_suite=suite,\n            task_id=task_id,\n            task_suite_name=suite_name,\n            camera_name=\"agentview_image,robot0_eye_in_hand_image\",\n            obs_type=\"pixels_agent_pos\",\n            observation_width=256,\n            observation_height=256,\n            control_mode=\"relative\",\n            init_states=True,\n            episode_index=0,\n            n_envs=1,\n        )\n\n        for ep in range(EPS_PER_TASK):\n            obs, info = env.reset(seed=42 + ep)\n            action_chunk = None\n            action_idx = 0\n            success = False\n\n            for step in range(max_steps):\n                if action_chunk is None or action_idx >= ACTION_HORIZON:\n                    batch = obs_to_batch(obs, task_text, tokenizer, device)\n                    action_chunk = predict_actions(policy, batch)\n                    action_idx = 0\n\n                action = action_chunk[action_idx].cpu().numpy().astype(np.float32)\n                action = np.clip(action, -1.0, 1.0)\n                action_idx += 1\n\n                obs, reward, terminated, truncated, info = env.step(action)\n\n                if info.get(\"is_success\", False):\n                    success = True\n                    break\n                if terminated or truncated:\n                    break\n\n            task_successes.append(success)\n            status = \"OK\" if success else \"FAIL\"\n            print(f\"  [{suite_name}] Task {task_id} Ep {ep+1}/{EPS_PER_TASK}: {status} (steps={step+1})\")\n\n        env.close()\n        sr = sum(task_successes) / len(task_successes)\n        results_per_task[f\"task_{task_id}\"] = {\n            \"task_text\": task_text.strip(),\n            \"success_count\": sum(task_successes),\n            \"n_episodes\": len(task_successes),\n            \"success_rate\": sr,\n        }\n        total_success += sum(task_successes)\n        total_eps += len(task_successes)\n        print(f\"  [{suite_name}] Task {task_id} SR: {sr:.0%} ({sum(task_successes)}/{len(task_successes)})\")\n\n    elapsed = time.time() - t0\n    overall_sr = total_success / total_eps if total_eps > 0 else 0.0\n    print(f\"\\n  [{suite_name}] OVERALL SR: {overall_sr:.1%} ({total_success}/{total_eps}) in {elapsed:.0f}s\")\n    return {\n        \"suite\": suite_name,\n        \"total_success\": total_success,\n        \"total_episodes\": total_eps,\n        \"overall_success_rate\": overall_sr,\n        \"per_task\": results_per_task,\n        \"elapsed_seconds\": elapsed,\n    }\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    device = torch.device(\"cuda:0\")\n    ckpt_path = PROJECT_DIR / \"checkpoints/libero_full_ft/step_099999/checkpoint.pt\"\n    config_path = str(PROJECT_DIR / \"configs/libero_full_ft.yaml\")\n\n    print(f\"Loading model from {ckpt_path}...\")\n    policy, config = load_model(str(ckpt_path), config_path, device)\n    tokenizer = get_tokenizer_from_policy(policy)\n    print(\"Model loaded.\\n\")\n\n    suites = [\"libero_object\", \"libero_spatial\", \"libero_goal\"]\n    all_results = {}\n\n    for suite_name in suites:\n        print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n        print(f\"Evaluating: {suite_name}\")\n        print(f\"{'='*60}\")\n        result = eval_suite(suite_name, policy, tokenizer, device)\n        all_results[suite_name] = result\n\n    del policy\n    torch.cuda.empty_cache()\n\n    out_path = PROJECT_DIR / \"eval_results\" / \"eval_fullft_100k_3suites.json\"\n    out_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    with open(out_path, \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(all_results, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n    print(\"SUMMARY — Full FT 100K (step_099999)\")\n    print(f\"{'='*60}\")\n    grand_total_s = 0\n    grand_total_e = 0\n    for sn, r in all_results.items():\n        print(f\"  {sn}: {r['overall_success_rate']:.1%} ({r['total_success']}/{r['total_episodes']})\")\n        grand_total_s += r['total_success']\n        grand_total_e += r['total_episodes']\n    avg_sr = grand_total_s / grand_total_e if grand_total_e > 0 else 0\n    print(f\"  AVERAGE: {avg_sr:.1%} ({grand_total_s}/{grand_total_e})\")\n    print(f\"\\nResults saved to {out_path}\")\nPYEOF\necho \"Script created.\"","account":"root"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1208,"cache_read_input_tokens":57052,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1208},"output_tokens":8,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"6fce3b75-6795-41b2-890d-533edd1dff4e","uuid":"094e7774-0b67-4d4e-b510-e48915e4ffe3"}
